# Complete source inventory

Upstream commit: `3aa0fa336c4a20e4149b6151629b6050897bb8a5`. Every tracked blob is bundled byte-for-byte under `source/`. Upstream symlinks are stored as inert text containing their exact link target; original modes are recorded in SOURCE-MANIFEST.json. This is an evidence bundle, not a deployment checkout.

Search paths here; then use the topical reference first. The symbol indexes are mechanically extracted navigation aids, not semantic audits or complete parsers.
